      var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments);

    }

  var promoCountdown = {
    Init:function(){
      var timer = $('.countdown');
      if (timer.length){
        timer.each(function(index){
          var timerCountDownTo = $(this).data('ends');
          var promoDeadlineDate = dateFns.parse(timerCountDownTo, "YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss", new Date());
          var promoDeadlineFormatted = dateFns.format(promoDeadlineDate, "YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss");
          var promoTextToPrepend = $(this).data('prepend') || '';
          var promoTextToAppend = $(this).data('append') || '';

          /*
            T3Core.Debug(index + ') timerCountDownTo: ' + timerCountDownTo);
            T3Core.Debug(index + ') promoTextToPrepend: ' + promoTextToPrepend);
            T3Core.Debug(index + ') promoTextToAppend: ' + promoTextToAppend);
          */

          $(this).countdown(promoDeadlineFormatted)
          .on('update.countdown', function(event) {
            if (event.offset.totalDays < 1) {
              format = '%-H:%M:%S';
              /*
                if(event.offset.totalHours > 0){
                  format = '%-Hh %Mm';
                } else {
                  format = '%Mm %Ss';
                }
              */
            } else {
              if ($(this).hasClass('expanded-days')){
                format = '%-D day%!d %-Hh %Mm %Ss';
              } else if ($(this).hasClass('digitale')){
                format = '%-D : %H : %M : %S';
              } else {
                format = '%-Dd %Hh %Mm %Ss';
              }
            }
            $(this).html(event.strftime(promoTextToPrepend + '<span class="timer">' + format + '</span>' + promoTextToAppend));
          })
        });
      }
    },
  };

  $(window).on('load', function(){
    var customDimensions = {
      SERVICE_WORKER_STATUS: 'dimension5'
    };
    var getSwStatus = function(){
      if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
        return navigator.serviceWorker.controller ? 'controlled' : 'supported';
      } else {
        return 'unsupported';
      }
    }
    var trackSwStatusTm;
    var trackSwStatus = function(){
      trackSwStatusTm && clearTimeout(trackSwStatusTm);
      if(typeof(ga) !== 'undefined'){
        var swStatus = getSwStatus();
        ga('set', customDimensions.SERVICE_WORKER_STATUS, swStatus);
        console.log('TSIO: sw ' + swStatus);
      } else {
        trackSwStatusTm = setTimeout(trackSwStatus, 1000);
      }
    };
    setTimeout(function(){
      trackSwStatus();
    }, 500);
